# 3D financial charts (graphics.el)

Generated by `make golden-3d` (`financial-chart-3d-write-gallery`); do not
edit by hand.

Every financial-chart template has a 3D variant in `templates3d/`: the
template with its body unchanged plus an `x-eas.graphics` naming a
[graphics.el](https://gitlab.com/davidawad/graphics-el) mark
(graphics.el's `docs/design/eas-integration.md`).
`src/financial-chart-3d.el` registers them with eas under the namespace
`fc3d`, so `fc3d/ohlc` is the variant of `ohlc`. eas still draws each in
2D; graphics.el draws it in 3D.

graphics.el is optional: without it everything 2D works as before and the
3D commands signal `financial-chart-3d-unavailable`, saying how to install
it.

```elisp
(require 'financial-chart-3d)
(financial-chart-3d-open "ohlc" :show t)                 ; its example
(financial-chart-3d-open "ohlc" :bindings '(:bars BARS) :show t)
M-x financial-chart-3d-gallery     ; every variant, a grid of windows
M-x financial-chart-3d-demo-book   ; the order-book surface, streaming
(setq v (financial-chart-3d-book-open SNAPSHOT :show t))  ; live book
(financial-chart-3d-book-push v DELTAS)
```

In a 3D buffer the arrows orbit, `+`/`-` zoom and `0` resets; a replay
plays and pauses with `SPC`, steps with `,` and `.` and changes speed
with `[` and `]`.

Marks: `bar3d` and `depth3d` are graphics.el's own. The `fc-` marks are
registered by `financial-chart-3d.el` with `graphics-eas-define-mark`:
candles whose scales span the whole series, stacked panes, a volume
profile beside candles, the underwater curve, a payoff heightfield, depth
walls and sign-coloured bars.

A replay draws every row at every moment: a series starts from its first
third in colour, the rest a dim ghost the replay colours bar by bar; a
payoff surface stays whole while a bright trace sweeps it to expiry. The
camera is fitted to the data (`financial-chart-3d-camera-fit`), so it
fills its pane at any size and holds still while a replay plays.

Each render below is the variant's golden (`test/golden/3d/NAME.txt`): its
example at 60x24 cells, dark palette, the fitted camera, at the end of its
replay, drawn by the Rust core; a replay's middle follows it
(`test/golden/3d/t0.5/NAME.txt`). The Braille text keeps the dots, not the
colours. `make test-3d` checks every variant against its template, draws
its goldens bit for bit and checks it reads at five sizes and five phases
(`test/financial-chart-3d-framing-test.el`).

| variant | mark | replay | 3D reading |
|---|---|---|---|
| [cds-term-structure](#cds-term-structure) | `fc-cds-term3d` | yes | the term structures as a surface of lines over tenor and date, coloured by level; replays date by date from its first eight, the dates to come a dim ghost, so a stress that flattens the front end stands out |
| [credit-spreads](#credit-spreads) | `fc-credit3d` |  | spread surface over tenor and rating as a wire mesh, AAA in front, log height, coloured by each spread's percentile in its own history (green tight, red wide) |
| [dcf-sensitivity](#dcf-sensitivity) | `fc-dcf3d` | yes | value per share as a surface over WACC and terminal growth, the current price a plane through it and the fair-value line where they cross; snapshots morph one into the next |
| [depth-live](#depth-live) | `depth3d` |  | order-book surface: cumulative depth by price (across) and step (back), streaming live from financial-chart-3d-book-push |
| [depth](#depth) | `fc-depth3d` |  | cumulative depth as two walls stepping away from the mid, bids in front, asks behind |
| [diverging-bars](#diverging-bars) | `fc-diverging3d` |  | one 3D bar per label, up from the floor for gains and down through it for losses |
| [drawdown](#drawdown) | `fc-drawdown3d` | yes | the underwater curve as a slab hanging below the high-water line, seen from below; replays from its first third point by point |
| [greeks-surface](#greeks-surface) | `fc-greeks3d` | yes | the greek as a surface over spot and days to expiry, the slice at the current spot traced; a bright trace sweeps it toward expiry |
| [kelly-surface](#kelly-surface) | `fc-kelly3d` |  | log growth as a surface over bet fraction and edge, the Kelly fraction traced along its ridge and the zero-growth line where over-betting starts to lose |
| [key-rate-durations](#key-rate-durations) | `fc-krd3d` |  | a bar per holding and key rate, as tall as its duration (short exposure below the floor), each holding its own colour |
| [ladder](#ladder) | `bar3d` |  | the price ladder as 3D bars, one per level, bids and asks in two lanes; live pushes grow and shrink them |
| [liquidity-heatmap](#liquidity-heatmap) | `fc-liquidity3d` | yes | Bookmap's heatmap raised: resting size by step (across) and price (away), dark blue to amber, the mid price traced in white; replays step by step, and streams live from financial-chart-3d-liquidity-push |
| [ohlc](#ohlc) | `fc-candle3d` | yes | candles as boxes along time, price up, each body as deep as its volume; replays from its first third bar by bar |
| [options-gex](#options-gex) | `fc-strikes3d` |  | dealer gamma as 3D bars on a strike by expiry floor, call gamma up and put gamma down, spot a plane across the strikes and the gamma flip (where net gamma by strike changes sign) a violet one |
| [options-oi](#options-oi) | `fc-strikes3d` |  | open interest as 3D bars on a strike by expiry floor, calls rising and puts hanging below it, spot a plane across the strikes and each expiry's max-pain strike a gold post |
| [options-skew](#options-skew) | `fc-skew3d` | yes | risk reversal (top) and butterfly (bottom) as ribbons over time, one per tenor (shortest in front), the newest term structure traced bright across them; replays snapshot by snapshot |
| [options-vrp](#options-vrp) | `fc-vrp3d` | yes | implied and realized volatility as the two edges of one ribbon over time, filled green where implied is rich and red where realized overtakes it, IV rank as bars behind and the front/back IV ratio in front; replays from its first third day by day |
| [panes](#panes) | `fc-panes3d` | yes | price, volume and RSI(14) as three stacked panels on one time axis; replays from its first third bar by bar |
| [payoff-curves](#payoff-curves) | `fc-payoff3d` | yes | the curves as one P/L surface over price and curve (a strategy at several dates: a surface toward expiry); a bright trace sweeps it curve by curve |
| [payoff](#payoff) | `fc-payoff3d` | yes | P/L surface over price and days to expiry, the earlier days priced from the expiry payoff; a bright trace sweeps it to expiry |
| [pop-surface](#pop-surface) | `fc-pop3d` |  | the probability as a surface over strike and expiry, red at 0 to green at 1, the even-odds level framed |
| [position-pnl-surface](#position-pnl-surface) | `fc-pnl3d` | yes | P/L surface over spot and days to expiry with the expiry payoff as its front edge; a bright trace sweeps it day by day toward it |
| [revenue-surface](#revenue-surface) | `fc-revenue3d` | yes | revenue as a surface over fiscal year and outcome quantile, the cone widening with the horizon and the median traced; it grows year by year from its first third (at least 8 years) |
| [risk-contribution](#risk-contribution) | `fc-risk-ribbons3d` | yes | one ribbon per asset, its height that asset's share of portfolio volatility; replays date by date from its first third, the dates to come a dim ghost |
| [risk-correlation](#risk-correlation) | `fc-risk-grid3d` | yes | the correlation matrix as signed 3D bars on an asset by asset floor (up green for positive, down red for negative), animated window by window so the bars rise and sink as correlations shift |
| [risk-factor-exposure](#risk-factor-exposure) | `fc-risk-ribbons3d` | yes | one ribbon per factor around a zero plane, long exposures standing above it and short ones hanging below; replays date by date from its first third, the dates to come a dim ghost |
| [risk-frontier](#risk-frontier) | `fc-risk-frontier3d` | yes | the frontiers as one surface of return over volatility (across) and date (back), coloured by Sharpe, each date's maximum-Sharpe portfolio marked |
| [risk-return-distribution](#risk-return-distribution) | `fc-risk-histogram3d` | yes | one histogram per horizon in lanes from front to back on shared return bins, loss bins red and gain bins green, each lane's 95% VaR ruled across it |
| [risk-stress](#risk-stress) | `fc-risk-grid3d` |  | the scenario by position grid as signed 3D bars, losses sinking red through the floor and gains rising green, a net column per scenario |
| [risk-underwater](#risk-underwater) | `fc-risk-ribbons3d` | yes | one slab per strategy hanging below its own high-water line, a ridgeline as deep as each drawdown, the worst point of each marked; replays week by week from its first third, the weeks to come a dim ghost |
| [rrg](#rrg) | `fc-rrg3d` | yes | the rotation graph on the floor (RS-Ratio across, RS-Momentum away), each sector's trail of its last tail dates climbing to its newest point, a stem down to where it stands now; rotates date by date |
| [sector-breadth](#sector-breadth) | `fc-breadth3d` | yes | breadth as a surface over sector (across) and date (toward the viewer), red to green, the 50% level framed, the newest date traced bright and the sectors' mean up the right wall; grows day by day |
| [session-profile](#session-profile) | `fc-session-profile3d` | yes | one volume-at-price slab per session, price across and sessions toward the viewer, each point of control capped white and the VWAPs joined across the floor; builds session by session |
| [vol-surface](#vol-surface) | `fc-vol-surface3d` | yes | IV surface over strike and days to expiry as a solid lit heightfield on every strike, coloured by IV, a light grid of a few smiles and term structures on it and the at-the-money term structure traced across it, each sample's tooltip its strike, expiry, IV, moneyness and contracts; replays the session quote time by quote time while the camera sways |
| [volume-profile](#volume-profile) | `fc-volume-profile3d` | yes | horizontal volume-at-price bars beside the candles, point of control bright; the profile builds as the bars replay from their first third |
| [yield-curve](#yield-curve) | `fc-yield-curve3d` | yes | the fitted curves as a surface of lines over tenor and date, each coloured by its regime (teal upward, amber flat, red inverted); replays curve by curve from its first eight, the curves to come a dim ghost, the forward curve beside the last |

## cds-term-structure

`fc3d/cds-term-structure`, mark `fc-cds-term3d`, example `test/fixtures/3d/rates/cds-term-structure.data.json`. The term structures as a surface of lines over tenor and date, coloured by level; replays date by date from its first eight, the dates to come a dim ghost, so a stress that flattens the front end stands out.

## greeks-surface

`fc3d/greeks-surface`, mark `fc-greeks3d`, example `examples/3d/greeks-surface.data.json`. The greek as a surface over spot and days to expiry, the slice at the current spot traced; a bright trace sweeps it toward expiry.

## ohlc

## options-vrp

`fc3d/options-vrp`, mark `fc-vrp3d`, example `test/fixtures/3d/options/vrp.data.json`. Implied and realized volatility as the two edges of one ribbon over time, filled green where implied is rich and red where realized overtakes it, IV rank as bars behind and the front/back IV ratio in front; replays from its first third day by day.

## payoff-curves

`fc3d/payoff-curves`, mark `fc-payoff3d`, example `examples/3d/payoff-curves.data.json`. The curves as one P/L surface over price and curve (a strategy at several dates: a surface toward expiry); a bright trace sweeps it curve by curve.

## payoff

`fc3d/payoff`, mark `fc-payoff3d`, example `examples/3d/payoff.data.json`. P/L surface over price and days to expiry, the earlier days priced from the expiry payoff; a bright trace sweeps it to expiry.

## risk-contribution

`fc3d/risk-contribution`, mark `fc-risk-ribbons3d`, example `test/fixtures/3d/risk/risk-contribution.json`. One ribbon per asset, its height that asset's share of portfolio volatility; replays date by date from its first third, the dates to come a dim ghost.

## risk-correlation

## risk-underwater

`fc3d/risk-underwater`, mark `fc-risk-ribbons3d`, example `test/fixtures/3d/risk/risk-underwater.json`. ## sector-breadth

`fc3d/sector-breadth`, mark `fc-breadth3d`, example `test/fixtures/3d/market/sector-breadth.data.json`. Breadth as a surface over sector (across) and date (toward the viewer), red to green, the 50% level framed, the newest date traced bright and the sectors' mean up the right wall; grows day by day.

## vol-surface

`fc3d/vol-surface`, mark `fc-vol-surface3d`, example `examples/vol-surface.data.json`. IV surface over strike and days to expiry as a solid lit heightfield on every strike, coloured by IV, a light grid of a few smiles and term structures on it and the at-the-money term structure traced across it, each sample's tooltip its strike, expiry, IV, moneyness and contracts; replays the session quote time by quote time while the camera sways.

## volume-profile

`fc3d/volume-profile`, mark `fc-volume-profile3d`, example `examples/volume-profile.data.json`. Horizontal volume-at-price bars beside the candles, point of control bright; the profile builds as the bars replay from their first third.

## yield-curve

`fc3d/yield-curve`, mark `fc-yield-curve3d`, example `test/fixtures/3d/rates/yield-curve.data.json`. The fitted curves as a surface of lines over tenor and date, each coloured by its regime (teal upward, amber flat, red inverted); replays curve by curve from its first eight, the curves to come a dim ghost, the forward curve beside the last.
